    <article class="markdown-body entry-content container-lg f5" itemprop="text"><div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 class="heading-element" dir="auto">Little brains in every product.</h1><a id="user-content-little-brains-in-every-product" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Little brains in every product." href="#little-brains-in-every-product"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>European AI lab building small, opinionated on-device models.</strong></p>
<p dir="auto">Every model is free up to 100k monthly active devices per SDK. Unlimited inference per user.</p>
<p dir="auto">Drop-in SDKs for iOS, Android, Web, and Node, with open weights on <a href="https://huggingface.co/desert-ant-labs" rel="nofollow">Hugging Face</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Try our models with the <a href="https://github.com/Desert-Ant-Labs/desert-ant-cli#desert-ant-cli">Desert Ant CLI</a>.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 class="heading-element" dir="auto">In closed beta</h3><a id="user-content-in-closed-beta" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: In closed beta" href="#in-closed-beta"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Weights exist and the models work, but no SDK ships them yet, so there is
nothing to install today. Ask us if you want early access.</p>
